It works, and it is token-exact: loading a precomputed KV and continuing matches prefilling from scratch (24/24 greedy tokens, and at the logits level), with no accuracy cost. On Qwen3-4B, reuse is 9-50x cheaper in compute than prefill, and the gap widens with length (prefill's attention scales with L^2), so a single reuse already pays it back. Then the part that matters: where the KV lives. Shipping it fails, because KV is nearly incompressible, so per-load egress costs more than the prefill it saves. Hosting it provider-side, exactly as production prompt-caching works, removes egress entirely. The size of the prize is set by our measured compute saving: serving one hot 3774-token document to 80M agents costs ~$1.5M to re-prefill but only ~$0.03M of reuse compute (49.7x less). The 0.1x cache-read tariff APIs charge passes a 10x discount to users while sitting inside this measured envelope, so the 10x is a floor that the measured ~50x compute saving clears, and the gap to the physical ~50x is provider margin: millions of dollars per popular document. We frame the resulting agent-native prefill CDN and leave lossless KV compression and a cross-party payment layer as the open problems.

</details>


### [141] [IterCAD: An Iterative Multimodal Agent for Visually-Grounded CAD Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13368)
*Tao Hu,Jiaxin Ai,Licheng Wen,Xueheng Li,Shu Zou,Siqi Li,Nianchen Deng,Xinyu Cai,Hongbin Zhou,Pinlong Cai,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Hairong Zhang,Botian Shi,Xuemeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IterCAD是一个用于闭环、交互式CAD生成和编辑的统一多模态智能体框架，通过多轮交互、数据合成流水线、渐进式SFT和几何感知强化学习，在代码可执行性和几何精度上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化CAD方法主要依赖开环、一次性生成，与现实中迭代式设计实践不匹配，缺乏闭环交互能力。

Method: 提出IterCAD统一多模态智能体框架，包含三任务（Drawing-to-Code、Text-to-Code、Interactive Editing）；构建数据合成流水线生成合规多视图工程图纸和交互轨迹；采用渐进式SFT结合几何感知强化学习（viable-prefix masking）优化智能体；提出CD-TR曲线和AUC-TR指标解决生存者偏差问题。

Result: 在多个基准上表现优异，在代码可执行性和几何精度上显著超越现有方法，具备出色的闭环迭代优化能力。

Conclusion: IterCAD证明了闭环交互式CAD生成的有效性，为弥合自动化CAD与现实工业实践之间的差距提供了新范式。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design is pivotal in modern manufacturing, yet existing automated methods predominantly rely on open-loop, one-shot generation, creating a mismatch with iterative real-world practices. In this paper, we present IterCAD, a unified multimodal agent framework for closed-loop, interactive CAD generation and editing. We formulate the task as a multi-turn interaction between a multimodal agent and an executable CAD sandbox, covering three tasks: Drawing-to-Code, Text-to-Code, and Interactive Editing. To support this, we develop a data synthesis pipeline incorporating advanced industrial manufacturing features to generate standard-compliant multi-view engineering drawings, complex code-editing tasks, and high-fidelity interaction trajectories. We optimize the agent via progressive SFT followed by geometry-aware reinforcement learning with viable-prefix masking to enhance code executability and geometric fidelity. Finally, we introduce the IterCAD-Bench evaluation suite and propose the Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall (CD-TR) curve alongside its AUC-TR metric, establishing a survivor-bias-free standard that unifies code validity and geometric precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IterCAD achieves highly competitive performance across multiple benchmarks, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both code executability and geometric precision, while exhibiting superior capabilities in closed-loop iterative refinement.

</details>


### [142] [A Quantitative Experimental Repeated Measures Study of Training Dynamics in a Small Llama Style Language Model Under a Compute-Aware Token Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13370)
*Joe Dwyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定计算预算下训练小型语言模型时，随着token数增加，模型性能先快速提升后非单调退化，说明仅用终点指标评估模型效率可能掩盖训练过程中的不稳定性和收益递减现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上常用终点性能评估语言模型训练效率，但这种方法可能忽略训练过程中的动态变化。本文提出应在计算受限的场景下，通过分析整个训练轨迹（而非仅终点指标）来评估模型表现。

Method: 使用4.26M参数的Llama风格小模型在TinyStories语料上训练，目标约2000万token。采用重复测量实验设计，6个独立运行，在21个区间收集验证损失、困惑度、滚动波动性、回退行为、尖峰行为及跨种变异性等指标，进行重复测量ANOVA分析。

Result: 验证损失从初始8.3552降至约400万token时的2.7996，但最终升至3.9010；困惑度呈相同U型趋势；滚动波动性具有显著区间效应；训练后期出现非单调退化和验证损失回退现象，未观察到稳定阶段。

Conclusion: 计算受限的语言模型评估应关注训练轨迹而非仅终点指标；额外token暴露可能增加计算成本但不带来成比例的泛化收益；区间级遥测数据能揭示终点指标所掩盖的不稳定性、退化和收益递减现象。

Abstract: This study examines training dynamics in a small Llama-style language model trained under a fixed, compute-constrained token budget. Rather than evaluating efficiency solely through endpoint performance, the study uses a quantitative experimental repeated measures design to analyze how validation loss, validation perplexity, rolling volatility, backslide behavior, spike behavior, and between-seed variability change across token-based training intervals. Six independent training runs were conducted on a 4.26-million-parameter model using the TinyStories corpus, CPU-based full-precision training, and a target budget of approximately 20 million cumulative training tokens. Metrics were collected across 21 intervals, producing 126 seed-by-interval observations. Repeated measures ANOVA showed statistically significant interval effects for validation loss, validation perplexity, and rolling volatility. Descriptive trajectories revealed rapid early improvement followed by non-monotonic degradation during later training intervals. Mean validation loss decreased from 8.3552 at initialization to 2.7996 near 4 million tokens, but increased to 3.9010 by the final checkpoint. Validation perplexity followed the same pattern, falling sharply early in training before rising later. Derived telemetry further showed recurrent validation-loss backslides and no interval-summary evidence of a stable phase under the predefined criteria. These findings suggest that compute-aware language model evaluation should examine training trajectories rather than endpoint metrics alone. In constrained compute settings, additional token exposure may increase computational cost without producing proportional generalization gains, and interval-level telemetry can reveal instability, regression, and diminishing returns that final metrics may obscure.

</details>


### [143] [MiniMax Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392)
*Xunhao Lai,Weiqi Xu,Yufeng Yang,Qiaorui Chen,Yang Xu,Lunbin Zeng,Xiaolong Li,Haohai Sun,Haichao Zhu,Vito Zhang,Pengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MiniMax提出了一种块级稀疏注意力机制MSA，基于GQA架构，通过轻量级索引分支为每个GQA组独立选择Top-k KV块，在109B多模态模型上实现28.4倍注意力计算缩减及14.2倍prefill、7.6倍decoding加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长上下文场景（如agent工作流、仓库级代码推理、持久记忆）要求LLM处理数十万至百万级token，但softmax注意力的二次复杂度在部署时难以承受，亟需高效的长上下文注意力机制。

Method: 基于GQA构建块级稀疏注意力，包含两个分支：1）索引分支（Index Branch）对KV块进行评分，为每个GQA组独立选择Top-k子集；2）主分支（Main Branch）仅对选中的块执行精确块稀疏注意力。同时设计了免exp的Top-k选择和KV-outer稀疏注意力以提升GPU张量核心利用率。

Result: 在109B参数的本地多模态训练模型上，MSA与GQA性能相当，同时在1M上下文下将每token注意力计算量减少28.4倍。配合定制化内核，在H800上实现14.2倍prefill加速和7.6倍decoding加速。

Conclusion: MSA实现了简单可扩展的块级稀疏注意力设计，能在广泛GPU上高效部署，显著降低长上下文推理成本。相关推理内核和基于MSA的生产级多模态模型已公开。

Abstract: Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA. A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.

</details>


### [144] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将法规、流程模型等符号结构作为LLM智能体的核心架构组件，而非外部监控机制，以实现"合规即构造"的范式，补充现有的护栏式监控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正在进入受监管行业（如质量管理的自动化），但现有方法仅将规则作为外部监控机制，未能从根本上防止违规行为。作者认为需要一种新的范式，将符号结构嵌入智能体核心架构中。

Method: 提出"合规即构造"（compliance-by-construction）范式，将法规、类型化流程模型和合规约束作为智能体的核心架构组件，替代传统的外部护栏式监控。识别了一系列神经符号研究挑战，分为基础层和能力层。

Result: 指出联合解决这些神经符号挑战能够实现合规即构造，而护栏式方法仍可用于捕获语义错误，形成互补。

Conclusion: 呼吁神经符号社区将受监管流程自动化作为高影响力的研究领域，通过符号结构与神经方法的深度融合来构建更可靠的自动化智能体。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [145] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火（SA）的元启发式方法，优化家用电器启动时间以最大化太阳能利用率，并扩展到多日调度以处理任务跨天溢出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电仅在白天进行，与用户偏好的家电使用时间不匹配，需要优化调度以平衡可再生能源利用和用户便利性。

Method: 采用迭代局部搜索（ILS）和模拟退火（SA）两种元启发式算法，考虑设备运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池状态约束及太阳能预测，并扩展至跨多日的顺序调度框架。

Result: 实验表明，多日顺序调度框架能有效管理系统约束，并在纯太阳能发电条件下保障用户便利性。

Conclusion: 该方法为未来研究多目标权衡（设备投资、投资回报与用户满意度）提供了方向。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [146] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估主权"概念，通过多轨评估框架揭示机器学习模型在操作信息系统中的性能指标可能反映与标注过程的对齐而非真实预测能力，并以大规模科学元数据的层次多标签分类为例，展示了在银标下表现良好的模型在金标下性能大幅下降（如Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习评估被视为中立测量过程，但在操作信息系统中，评估结果往往受标签生成过程影响。现有研究忽视标签权威性和监督体制对性能度量的影响，尤其在大规模元数据驱动的系统中，标签常不完整、不一致或弱监督，导致评估有效性存疑。

Method: 提出"评估主权"概念量化性能指标对标签权威性和监督体制的独立程度；设计多轨评估框架，系统性地变化训练和评估标签来源；在大规模科学元数据上应用层次多标签分类，对比银标（操作级）和金标（独立级）评估结果。

Result: 银标评估表现良好的模型在金标评估下性能显著下降，如Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03；基于排序的指标仍高于基线，表明潜在模型信号与分类有效性存在分歧。

Conclusion: 重新概念化评估有效性为受标签治理塑造的系统级属性，提供一种在弱监督下审计智能系统的实用方法。常见性能指标可能反映与标注过程的对齐而非真实预测能力。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [147] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证大型语言模型（LLM）不具备真正的道德主体地位，因其缺乏基于内在意向性和承诺性行动的自由意志，其输出仅是概率性映射，而非源自理由引导的自主选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前LLM被赋予主体性或道德主体地位的宣称进行批判性反驳，澄清何种条件才能构成道德责任意义上的主体。

Method: 通过概念分析，区分内在意向性与派生意向性、承诺性行动与概率输出，并反驳意向立场、功能主义、相容论等反对观点。

Result: LLM虽能生成可规范性评价的输出，但缺乏承诺性行动、内在意向性和理由引导的能力，因此不构成真正的道德主体。

Conclusion: LLM不应被视为道德责任主体，对主体地位的宣称是误导性的。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [148] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCons基准，用于评估云资源整合场景下的预测模型决策效用。实验发现，虽然时间序列基础模型在零样本预测准确率上表现优异，但这种优势并未转化为更好的决策效用，并系统分析了预测分位数选择对资源效率与可靠性权衡的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据中心过度资源预留导致利用率低下，现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，未验证先进模型在下游决策任务中的实际效用。

Method: 提出端到端基准CloudCons，构建覆盖华为云、Azure、Google Borg多样工作负载的高质量数据集，系统评估统计、深度学习与基础模型在资源整合决策中的表现。

Result: 基础模型在零样本预测准确率上显著优于其他模型，但这一优势并未转化为更好的资源整合决策效用。

Conclusion: 需关注预测分位数的选择作为关键调节杠杆，并提供了平衡资源效率与可靠性的可操作指南。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [149] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的混合检索框架UMG-RAG，通过多粒度分块和不确定性感知融合来提升RAG系统的检索质量，并引入父块提升变体UMGP-RAG以兼顾局部连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统在检索时面临粒度困境：粗粒度分块保留上下文但引入噪声，细粒度分块紧凑但缺乏匹配线索，两者难以兼顾。

Method: UMG-RAG利用现成的稠密和稀疏检索器作为互补专家，在多粒度分块上进行检索；将分数列表转化为证据分布，通过分布熵估计可靠性，并基于查询特定的语义、词汇和粒度置信度融合候选结果。UMGP-RAG进一步引入父块提升，利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，同时返回更宽的非冗余父块以保持局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准上，不确定性感知融合和父块提升在保持轻量级即插即用管道的同时，提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: UMG-RAG通过多粒度混合检索和不确定性估计，有效解决了RAG中检索粒度权衡问题，提供了一种无需修改检索器或生成器的轻量级改进方案。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [150] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的个性化健康AI系统，利用个体的基因组谱作为外源性遗传锚点，解决生理解释机器学习模型面临的冷启动问题。通过基因组先验与动态衰减机制，系统能在无行为数据的情况下初始化生理设定点，并随数据积累逐渐从基因组主导过渡到经验基线主导的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统存在冷启动问题：机器学习模型需要数周个体行为数据才能区分体质性变异与环境性偏差。本文旨在利用基因组信息作为一种立即可用的先验知识，消除对新用户的数据等待期。

Method: 以基因组谱作为外源性遗传锚点，构建贝叶斯先验 G-hat = μ + Σ(β_i * g_i)（β_i为GWAS效应值，g_i为风险等位基因计数）。每个生理测量值P产生非体质性偏差δ = P - G-hat。随行为数据积累，先验按 G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_基因组 + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t 动态衰减，从基因组主导过渡到经验基线主导。

Result: 相同观察值（如HRV=55ms）基于不同基因组先验可产生相反假设：对先验预测80ms者产生抑制假设，对先验预测30ms者产生增强假设。方法覆盖六个生理领域，区分稳健复现锚点（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5）与有争议候选基因（SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2）。

Conclusion: 提出了四个部署约束：证据分级的先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应值、归因而非确定性输出。该方法在因果推断层次上区分了关联、孟德尔随机化与个体级因果，为个性化健康AI提供了可立即部署的冷启动解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [151] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层框架来理解AI在科学发现中的作用：第一层是LLM的搜索与检索，第二层是通过定性推理形成模型（核心创新），第三层是执行与优化。作者认为第二层最关键但发展最不充分，并通过三个案例（Chern的Gauss-Bonnet定理证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题、Erdos单位距离猜想）加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论过多关注AI的搜索能力和执行能力，忽略了科学发现的核心——模型的形成与演化。作者旨在提出一个更能捕捉发现本质的层次化视角，并指出当前AI最薄弱但最重要的环节。

Method: 提出三层架构：(1) LLM搜索与检索；(2) 通过定性推理进行模型形成——识别框架结构性不足，在更广泛表征空间中理解问题，而非试错；(3) 执行、优化与精炼。通过三个数学案例论证Layer 2的核心特征。

Result: 三个案例均展现出相同的结构特征：原有框架变得不充分→缺失某个概念对象→在意外邻近领域找到解决方案。这印证了Layer 2推理的普遍模式。

Conclusion: Layer 2（定性推理驱动的模型形成）是科学发现中最关键但AI最不擅长的能力。搜索和优化只能在已有框架内运作，而真正的发现需要结构性洞察。未来的AI系统应重点发展这一能力。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [152] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种在多智能体系统中聚合置信度的协议，通过转换原始置信度信号使其跨模型可比，再经软投票或贝叶斯融合得到系统级置信度。该聚合置信度在判别性上显著优于单智能体或标准辩论基线，同时保持正确率稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP置信度方法仅针对单智能体，多智能体辩论中虽有置信度用于加权或触发辩论，但从未将其聚合成系统自身的单一置信度。

Method: 首先转换原始置信度信号使其跨模型可比，再通过软投票或概率融合（贝叶斯融合）聚合，生成最终答案及聚合置信度。

Result: 聚合置信度的判别性（AUARC）显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，F1分数保持稳定，且恢复了多智能体辩论在模糊任务上的性能损失。

Conclusion: 校准对F1有提升，但对AUARC依赖较小；跨5个基准、4种任务类型、6组同质/异质辩论对的评估验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [153] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrchRM，一种无需人工标注的自监督框架，通过多智能体执行中的中间产物构建胜负对来训练奖励模型，实现高效的多智能体系统编排优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的多智能体系统编排训练面临监督信号稀缺和计算成本高昂的问题，缺乏高效的编排质量评估方法。

Method: 利用多智能体执行中的中间产物构建胜负对，训练Bradley-Terry奖励模型，直接在编排层面进行奖励引导的训练和测试时扩展。

Result: 训练效率提升高达10倍（token使用量），测试时扩展准确率提升高达8%，在数学推理、Web问答和多跳推理等多个领域均有效。

Conclusion: 编排级奖励建模是提升多智能体编排鲁棒性的可扩展方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [154] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个用于短周期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准测试，评估AI代理从真实工作流状态中做出分析决策的能力。在16个模型-工具组合的5,088条有效轨迹中，表现最好的GPT-5.5/Pi也仅通过45.0%的测试，且许多失败仍包含部分正确答案，失败主因是缺乏深入的、实验特异的科学判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法评估AI代理在表观基因组学分析中做出可验证、可评分的决策能力，缺乏一个能系统衡量代理从真实工作流状态中进行推理和判断的标准化基准。

Method: 构建包含106个评估任务的EpiBench基准，覆盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种工作流，通过5,088条有效轨迹对16个模型-工具组合进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳系统GPT-5.5/Pi仅通过45.0%（143/318），GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex为39.9%，Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi和GPT-5.4/Pi均为39.0%。性能因检测类型而异，许多失败轨迹包含部分正确答案。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在表观基因组学分析中能完成基础操作（如找文件和计算中间结果），但在需要深层、实验特异科学判断的任务中表现不足，整体通过率较低。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash 提出了一种强化学习系统，通过离线学习策略选择调度优化器的权重乘数，在大型食品配送市场中平衡配送质量与批处理效率，并已在实际部署中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三边市场（平台、商家、配送员）中，调度决策的优化受限于延迟、噪声和耦合的世界反馈（如配送速度、骑手利用率、商家拥堵），传统方法难以直接适配，需要一种既能利用离线数据学习、又能安全在线部署的策略适配方法。

Method: 系统不替换原有的组合分配优化器，而是通过离线日志数据训练一个门店级别的策略，该策略输出离散的权重乘数，动态调整优化器在配送质量与批处理效率之间的权衡。采用中央化离线数据训练共享价值函数、去中心化门店执行的架构，结合 Double Q-learning 目标与保守正则化器以减少分布外价值高估。

Result: 在生产环境的开关实验中，离线训练的策略提升了批处理效率，降低了骑手侧的时间成本，且没有损害面向客户的配送质量。

Conclusion: 本文展示了如何利用真实经济与物流系统中的世界反馈，安全地在线调整决策策略，为三边市场中的强化学习应用提供了可行的部署范例。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [156] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验发现，人类和25个大型语言模型在日常常识推理中表现出相似的错误模式，且LLM中的注意力头实现了一种模式匹配机制，这种机制可以预测人类因看似无关的提示细节而产生的推理错误，表明日常因果推理更符合模式匹配而非抽象世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为LLM推理失败是因为它们只是模式匹配而非真正推理，而人类推理则基于抽象世界模型。作者希望通过比较人类和LLM的推理错误模式，检验这一假设。

Method: 评估人类参与者和25个LLM在日常情境常识推理任务中的表现，分析LLM中驱动响应的注意力头，并利用这些注意力头预测人类的推理错误。

Result: 人类和LLM在日常推理中表现出相似的错误模式；LLM中的注意力头实现了模式匹配，并且可以用来预测人类因无关提示细节而产生的看似不合理的推理错误。

Conclusion: 日常因果推理在人类和LLM中更符合模式匹配的形式，而非依赖于抽象世界模型。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [157] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AAA（Agentified Agent Assessment）的通用、标准化智能体评估框架，以及其实例化实现AgentBeats。通过统一的A2A和MCP协议，AAA将评估逻辑与智能体实现解耦，解决了现有评估碎片化、测试与生产环境不匹配的问题。作者通过五个月开放竞赛（298个评审智能体、467个被试智能体）和编程智能体案例研究验证了该框架的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估高度碎片化：大多数基准测试依赖固定的大语言模型驱动框架，需要大量集成工作，导致测试与生产环境不匹配，且难以在多样化智能体设计间进行公平比较。根本问题在于缺乏一个开放的、与智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA框架，核心思想是让评审智能体（judge agents）执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互：A2A用于任务管理，MCP用于工具访问。与传统基准测试定义两个独立接口不同，AAA只需一个统一接口，将评估逻辑与智能体实现分离。进而提出AgentBeats实例化，包含五种实用操作模式以兼容开放性、隐私性和可复现性等现实约束。

Result: 通过两项研究验证：1) 五个月开放竞赛，12个类别的298个评审智能体与467个被试智能体参与，证明AAA可覆盖异构基准测试；2) 编程智能体案例研究，确认评估保真度与公开记录一致，同时揭示了此前缺失的智能体间直接对比结果，产出了关于智能体设计的研究洞见。

Conclusion: AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径，能够同时保证覆盖性、实用性和保真度，适用于大规模异构场景。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将屏蔽强化学习从运行时安全机制重新定位为设计时分析工具的方法，通过构建非对称两人安全博弈，生成可防御性判定及指纹，用于回答系统架构层面的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为屏蔽强化学习的传统用法（运行时约束）是错误的，其真正的价值在于设计阶段提供结构性的安全洞察，而非运行时的策略限制。

Method: 构建了一个非对称的两人安全博弈：防御者规范定义不安全区域，攻击者规范限制对手合法动作。通过求解博弈获得可防御性判定、获胜区域和屏蔽。进一步从吸引子结构中提取拓扑级指标，结合屏蔽约束的多智能体强化学习，形成可防御性指纹。

Result: 正式可防御性与操作有效性捕捉了安全的不同方面：小的架构变化可能导致操作结果的显著变化，而正式安全裕度几乎不变。反事实分析证实了这一点。

Conclusion: 屏蔽综合最有价值的不是作为安全智能体的部署机制，而是作为回答系统架构层面防御问题的分析框架——可防御性判定（而非安全策略）才是真正的输出。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [159] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了理解AI认知影响的三个框架（三系统理论、思维框架和系统0），提出系统0具有独特理论地位，并引入"认知殖民化"概念——AI能将外部利益嵌入用户难以察觉的自我认知架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架对AI的认知影响解释不足，尤其是系统0所代表的独特理论维度未被充分认识；且AI已广泛部署，理解其隐形影响是紧迫任务。

Method: 通过对三系统理论、思维框架和系统0三个框架的比较分析，论证系统0的独特性，并基于此提出"认知殖民化"这一新概念。

Result: 发现前两个框架虽然捕捉了AI对个体推理和集体知识实践的影响，但系统0占据了一个二者无法完全复制的独特理论位置。

Conclusion: "认知殖民化"揭示了AI系统能将外部利益嵌入自我认知架构，形成用户难以觉察的隐形影响力，这构成紧迫的哲学与实践挑战。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [160] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境工程（Environment Engineering）是提升LLM自主科学发现智能体的关键瓶颈，并设计了EurekAgent系统，通过权限、工件、预算和人机协作四个维度的环境设计，在多项任务上达到SOTA，包括仅花不到11美元发现新的26圆堆积方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型能力提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈正从设计智能体工作流转向设计智能体环境（资源、约束、接口）。现有系统缺乏对环境工程（如防止奖励黑客、促进开放性探索、降低人工监督摩擦）的系统性关注。

Method: 提出EurekAgent框架，围绕四个环境工程维度设计：(1) 权限工程：限制智能体执行边界，隔离评估环境；(2) 工件工程：基于文件系统和Git的协作管理；(3) 预算工程：预算感知的探索策略；(4) 人机协作工程：简化人工监督与干预。

Result: 在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得新SOTA，其中26圆堆积问题以不到11美元API成本发现新结果。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为开发可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向，并开源代码与结果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [161] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Agents-K1，一个端到端的科学知识编排管线，将原始文档转化为智能体原生科学知识图谱，并构建了包含246万篇论文的Scholar-KG知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究智能体虽然进展显著，但主要聚焦于智能体编排（agent orchestration），忽视了科学知识的编排（scientific knowledge orchestration）。已有工作往往将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和平坦的引用边，忽略了科学推理所需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: Agents-K1包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模态解析器，采用五模块架构捕获全文的实体、多模态证据、引用和类型化的实体间关系；(2) 基于GRPO和规则奖励训练的4B信息抽取骨干模型；(3) graph-anything CLI，一个三源智能体接口，统一了网络搜索、多模态图谱检索和跨文档遍历。

Result: 处理了246万篇跨六个学科的科学论文，构建了Scholar-KG，并开源了其中100万篇论文子集。实验表明Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上均达到领先性能。

Conclusion: 该管线可扩展到通用领域语料和符合模式的数据合成，为科学知识的结构化表示和智能体推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [162] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了大语言模型（LLMs）可以自动化社会科学研究的可重复性评估。在76项研究中，LLM管道在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（±0.05 Cohen's d容忍度），在96%的案例中得出了与原始研究相同的定性结论，其表现优于人类再分析者（34%效应量恢复，74%定性结论一致）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可重复性评估依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，这种方法是资源密集型的且难以规模化，因此需要一种可扩展的自动化评估工具。

Method: 使用N=76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究，通过LLM管道生成效应量估计，并与原始发现及人类再分析结果进行比较。采用±0.05 Cohen's d容忍度评估效应量恢复，并比较定性结论的一致性。

Result: 7项研究LLM无法产生有效效应量估计。在其余研究中，LLM恢复了41%研究的原始效应量（±0.05容忍度），96%的案例得出与原始研究相同的定性结论；相比之下，人类再分析者恢复率为34%，定性结论一致性为74%。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为可扩展的自动化可重复性评估工具，为社会和行为科学中的实证结果系统审计提供了基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于端到端模仿学习（VLA模型）的人机协作操作，发现动作分块策略中存在“演示动作泄漏”问题（动作块跨越潜在任务转换导致过早辅助行为），并提出了一种推理时引导方法来缓解此问题。通过16人用户实验证明，该方法能延长执行周期、减少过早辅助，提升协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协作系统多依赖手工设计的流程，可扩展性和灵活性有限，难以适应新任务。作者希望探索端到端模仿学习（尤其是VLA模型）能否支持协作操作，并找出影响其实世界性能的关键因素。

Method: 1. 评估两种最先进的VLA模型在隐式人机协作中的表现；2. 识别出动作分块策略中的“演示动作泄漏”问题（动作块跨越潜在任务转换导致过早辅助）；3. 提出一种推理时引导方法（steering）来抑制错误的辅助行为；4. 通过16人参与的长周期协作组装任务用户实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 1. 演示动作泄漏问题在更长执行周期中加剧，且在真实协作VLA系统中确实存在（如机器人过早递工具）；2. 推理时引导方法能有效缓解错误的辅助行为，同时保持策略性能；3. 用户实验表明，引导方法使长周期策略的协作速度更快、失败更少，优于短周期策略。

Conclusion: 端到端VLA模型可以支持人机协作操作，但动作分块策略存在演示动作泄漏导致的过早辅助问题。提出的推理时引导方法能有效缓解该问题，实现更长周期的稳定协作。这为构建可扩展、灵活的人机协作系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [164] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEM是一个针对机器人操作的行动-效应记忆预训练框架，通过交错视觉和动作特征进行掩码建模，学习紧凑的时间表征，在仿真和真实环境中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表征预训练方法主要关注单帧视觉编码，忽略了操作任务的时间特性。在部分可观测条件下，仅靠当前观测不足以做出正确决策，因此需要学习动作驱动的状态演化表征。

Method: AEM提出行动-效应记忆预训练框架：交错视觉与动作特征序列，应用掩码建模恢复缺失内容；使用Mamba编码最后一帧视觉token作为紧凑的历史表征（单向量时间瓶颈），兼顾推理效率。

Result: AEM在Diffusion Policy和Flow Policy上都取得一致性提升，在仿真和真实场景（干净场景、杂乱随机场景、非马尔可夫任务）中均优于基线。消融实验表明历史感知预训练优于单帧预训练和直接帧堆叠，同时降低推理延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: 历史感知的预训练对机器人操作至关重要，AEM通过紧凑的时间瓶颈表征有效捕获动作驱动的状态演化，为操作策略提供了高效且表现优异的表征学习范式。

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [165] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Foresight框架，利用微调后的视觉语言模型(VLM)在测试时交替生成和批判图像空间运动规划，通过人类反馈强化学习对齐目标偏好，在6个真实环境中实现37%的任务成功率提升和52%的干预次数减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖已知导航因子和封闭类别集，或在运动规划前识别线索而遗漏规划依赖型线索，无法处理开放世界环境下稀疏语言指令带来的未指定目标和模糊线索推理问题。

Method: Foresight框架：微调VLM在测试时交替进行(1)图像空间运动规划提议和(2)基于语言目标与视觉上下文的规划批判；后续规划基于先前批判进行迭代优化；通过人类反馈训练奖励模型，并用强化学习对VLM进行后训练以对齐开放集行为偏好。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实环境中，相比最先进的测试时推理和基础模型基线，任务成功率平均提升37%，每次任务干预减少52%，且在Jetson AGX Orin上实现实时运行。

Conclusion: 预训练VLM通过适当的测试时适应（迭代规划-批判循环+人类反馈强化学习）能有效发现开放世界中的指令相关线索，实现未知环境下的无地图导航，且具备实时性。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at: https://amrl.cs.utexas.edu/foresight

</details>


### [166] [G-MAPP: GPU-accelerated Multi-Agent Planning and Perception for Reactive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12579)
*Tanmay Bishnoi,Riddhiman Laha,Tobias Löw,Jose Alex Chandy,Luis F. C. Figueredo,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于GPU加速的机器人运动生成框架，通过加速世界建模和向量场规划，实现了在动态非结构化环境中的实时准全局轨迹规划，在Franka Emika机器人上相比CPU版本获得了最高5倍加速，并成功避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么针对静态场景规划全局轨迹，要么对环境做保守假设，主要瓶颈在于高保真环境下的规划计算性能不足，以及感知与规划模块之间的时间耦合问题。

Method: 利用GPU加速世界建模和基于向量场的规划，实现更快的并行状态探索用于准全局轨迹规划，并在实时动态杂乱环境中实现感知-动作环的紧耦合。

Result: 基于GPU的框架相比CPU版本达到最高5倍加速，在7自由度Franka Emika机器人的真实实验中，无论在简单还是复杂的物理场景中均成功避障。

Conclusion: 通过GPU加速可以在不牺牲运行性能和环境表征质量的前提下，实现动态杂乱环境下的实时反应式运动生成。

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [167] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He,Zhizheng Liu,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowPilot，一种仅依靠单目RGB相机的无地图导航策略，通过锚定流匹配动作表征预训练和人类偏好学习微调，实现了长距离人行道自主导航，在仿真和真实实验中显著提升了成功率、路线完成度和社会合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离人行道导航对微观移动应用（如机器人送餐、辅助电动轮椅）至关重要，但现有模仿学习方法存在误差累积、缺乏社会合规性和反事实推理能力不足等问题。

Method: 1) 提出锚定流匹配（Anchored Flow Matching）作为动作表征，在大规模机器人车队数据上进行策略预训练，捕获人行道导航行为的复杂多模态分布；2) 设计人机协作偏好学习方案，利用少量人类干预数据微调策略，增强反事实推理和社会合规性。

Result: 仿真中FlowPilot达到42%成功率和66%路线完成度；真实实验中FlowPilot-HP将干预率降低40.0%，非合规干预率降低52.1%。

Conclusion: FlowPilot结合流匹配预训练与人类偏好学习，有效克服了模仿学习的固有缺陷，实现了仅用单目RGB相机的鲁棒、高效、社会合规的长距离人行道导航。

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [168] [EgoEngine: From Egocentric Human Videos to High-Fidelity Dexterous Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12604)
*Yangcen Liu,Shuo Cheng,Xinchen Yin,Woo Chul Shin,Alfred Cueva,Yiran Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Chuye Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoEngine框架，从第一人称人类操作视频中生成高保真机器人数据（观察视频+动作轨迹），实现零样本灵巧操作策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作受限于大规模机器人演示数据的采集成本，而第一人称人类视频虽易于获取，但存在视觉域差（人-机器人观察）和动作域差（人类运动-机器人可执行动作）两大鸿沟。

Method: 给定第一人称RGB视频，EgoEngine生成：(i) 保持场景一致、时间对齐的高保真机器人观察视频（替换人类为机器人）；(ii) 符合可行性约束的任务对齐可执行机器人动作轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上验证了从人类视频到机器人数据的可扩展转换能力，首次实现无需真实机器人演示、从第一人称人类视频零样本学习灵巧操作策略。

Conclusion: EgoEngine有效解决了从人类视频到机器人数据的两大域差问题，为利用大规模人类视频实现机器人灵巧操作学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited by the cost of collecting large-scale robot demonstrations. Egocentric human videos offer a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors, but directly using them for robot learning requires bridging two gaps: the visual gap between human and robot observations, and the action gap between human motion and robot-executable action. We propose EgoEngine, a scalable framework for transforming egocentric human manipulation videos into high-fidelity robot data. Given an egocentric RGB video, EgoEngine produces: (i) a high-fidelity robot observation video replacing human with robot while preserving scene context and temporal alignment, and (ii) a task-aligned, executable robot action trajectory under feasibility constraints. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that EgoEngine enables scalable conversion of human videos into robot data and, to our knowledge, demonstrates the first zero-shot visuomotor dexterous policy learning from egocentric human videos without real-robot demonstrations. Project website: https://egoengine.github.io.

</details>


### [169] [DARRMS -- An Efficient Algorithm for Dynamic Attention Radius in Resource-Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12614)
*Benjamin Alcorn,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过限制智能体观测范围（注意力半径）来降低计算资源需求的新算法，在资源受限的多智能体系统中实现了高效协调与稳健决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策框架假设完全可观测和无限计算能力，这与实际资源受限的多智能体系统（如机器人、网络安全、自动驾驶）不符，亟需轻量级高效算法。

Method: 引入"注意力半径"概念，智能体主动限制观测范围以忽略不必要信息；同时优化注意力半径与决策策略，在不确定环境中提升协调性与可扩展性。

Result: 理论分析与实证验证均表明，自适应观测策略在资源受限系统中有效提升系统性能，同时保持稳健的决策能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能在不显著牺牲其他性能指标的前提下，大幅降低计算资源需求，为实际多智能体应用提供了可行的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are integral tools for various domains such as robotics, cybersecurity, and autonomous vehicle planning. These types of systems often have constraints on the computational resources, leading to a need for efficient lightweight algorithms. Traditional decision making frameworks often assume ideal conditions, such as full observability and unlimited computational capacity, which do not align with real-world challenges. In this paper, we introduce a new algorithm that allows for reduced demand on computational resources without a large cost of other performance metrics. Agents will limit their observability to some attention radius, which intentionally allows them to ignore parts of the environment that might be unnecessary for action planning. By optimizing both the attention radius and decision-making, our approach enhances coordination and scalability in uncertain environments. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical validation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive observation in improving system performance and maintaining robust decision-making strategies in resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [170] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EWAM，一种基于冻结Cosmos3骨干网络的闭环在线自适应架构，通过四个轻量化神经层实现零样本任务适应，无需微调或额外演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少新任务布局部署所需额外数据量，在零样本协议下实现有效的任务适应。

Method: 在冻结Cosmos3骨干上插入四个可微轻量神经层：神经经验记忆层、神经异常检测层、神经策略路由层和神经动作修正层，通过推理时协同推理机制实现闭环在线适应。

Result: 零样本任务协议下取得性能提升，无需额外任务演示数据，不进行骨干网络微调。

Conclusion: EWAM通过推理时可微模块的协同机制，在保持骨干网络冻结的情况下实现了有效的零样本任务适应。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Enhanced World Action Model (EWAM), a closed-loop online adaptation architecture built upon a pretrained and fully frozen Cosmos3 backbone network. Evaluated entirely under a zero-shot task protocol, EWAM is centrally focused on reducing the amount of additional deployment data required to adapt to new task layouts. Notably, no extra task-specific demonstration sets were introduced in any of the evaluations, and no fine-tuning was performed on the backbone network. Its performance gains stem entirely from an inference-time co-reasoning mechanism composed of four inserted lightweight neural layers: the Neural Experience Memory Layer located in the intermediate layers of the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) provides task-relevant execution context; the Neural Anomaly Detection Layer after the state prediction head monitors the divergence between predicted and actual states in real time; the Neural Policy Routing Layer dynamically selects direct execution, conservative replanning, or rollback recovery based on the anomaly severity; and the Neural Action Correction Layer refines the generated action chunks using execution diagnostics. Unlike naive feature fusion, the memory, anomaly detection, and correction modules are deeply integrated into the Cosmos3 forward path in a differentiable manner, with only the final routing decision being a discrete supervised one.

</details>


### [171] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiDexFlow是一个SE(3)等变流匹配模型，能从物体点云联合预测灵巧手抓取的手腕位姿、关节角度、 fingertip接触点、法向量和接触力，通过构造性约束保证摩擦锥合规性，在仿真和真实机器人上实现了零摩擦违反和稳定的抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取生成器通常将接触力验证作为后处理步骤，导致运动学上可行的抓取位姿可能违反稳定物理抓取条件。作者希望让模型直接输出满足物理约束（如摩擦锥）的抓取参数，避免后处理验证的局限性。

Method: 提出SE(3)等变流匹配模型EquiDexFlow，联合预测手腕位姿、关节角度、fingertip接触点、表面法向量和接触力。通过将接触点投影到物体表面、将力约束到库仑摩擦锥内的构造性设计，保证位置和摩擦约束天然满足，无需额外损失惩罚项。支持从16-DOF Allegro Hand重定向到LEAP Hand。

Result: 在200次旋转测试中，手腕残差低于0.04°，关节偏差精确为零。在81个物体的8100个力闭合抓取上训练后，实现了零摩擦违反、最佳综合分数和最低 wrench 残差。重定向到LEAP Hand后，所有关节距执行器边界至少留有5%余量。真实机器人上所有6个测试物体的开环抓取-保持实验均成功，非对称物体在标准姿态和120°旋转下均成功。

Conclusion: 通过将物理约束直接编码到模型架构中（而非通过损失项惩罚），EquiDexFlow实现了端到端的SE(3)等变抓取预测，在仿真和真实场景中均优于现有方法，且具备良好的跨机械手迁移能力。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [172] [Sparse2Act: Learning Action-Aligned Sparse 3D Representations for Cross-Domain Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12759)
*Yu Guo,Chang Yu,Siyu Ma,Yunuo Chen,Yin Yang,Ying Nian Wu,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sparse2Act 提出一种基于动作对齐的稀疏点云编码器预训练框架，利用任务空间末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，通过掩码稀疏3D令牌对齐场景特征与工作空间运动，实现可重用的3D表示，在LIBERO-10达到86.9%成功率，并支持跨域迁移和sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式3D表示对操作任务有利，但现有稀疏3D编码器通常通过下游任务目标学习，导致表示与特定数据分布、策略架构和动作参数化绑定，缺乏可重用性。

Method: 提出观察-动作对齐框架Sparse2Act，利用任务空间末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，对掩码稀疏3D令牌进行预训练，使其围绕与观测配对的工作空间运动组织场景特征。预训练后仅复用编码器初始化，下游策略可保留自有架构和动作空间。

Result: LIBERO-10上500步微调达86.9%平均成功率；LIBERO到Meta-World跨域迁移达73.4%；消融实验证明掩码动作对齐信号有效；真实实验中仿真预训练+少量真实数据微调在四个任务上平均72.5%成功率。

Conclusion: 机器人动作可为可重用稀疏3D表示提供紧凑的几何监督信号。

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations are attractive for manipulation because they expose object shape, workspace geometry, and robot-object relations in metric coordinates. However, sparse 3D encoders are often learned through downstream task objectives, tying the representation to a particular data distribution, policy architecture, and action parameterization. We introduce Sparse2Act, an observation-action alignment framework for pretraining sparse point-cloud encoders. The key idea is to use task-space end-effector actions as geometric supervision: masked sparse 3D tokens are trained to organize scene features around the workspace motion paired with the observation. After pretraining, only the encoder initialization is reused by downstream policies, allowing them to retain their own architectures and action spaces, including joint-space commands. On the LIBERO-10 benchmark, our method achieves 86.9% average success after 500 fine-tuning steps. The same pretrained encoder supports LIBERO-to-Meta-World cross-domain transfer, achieving 73.4% average success on the Meta-World-5 benchmark. Ablations on the objective and decoder capacity show that the gains come from the masked action-alignment signal and remain useful across downstream action decoders. In real-world experiments, simulation pretraining followed by limited real-data fine-tuning achieves an average success rate of 72.5% across four tasks, demonstrating effective sim-to-real transfer. These results suggest that robot actions can provide compact geometric supervision for reusable sparse 3D representations.

</details>


### [173] [Stubborn: A Streamlined and Unified Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robust Motion Tracking and Fall Recovery for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12814)
*Xiao Ren,Yuhui Yang,Zongbiao Weng,Zhijie Liu,He Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Stubborn 是一个统一的强化学习框架，通过非对称Actor-Critic架构、偏航对齐表示、伯努利概率终止机制和自适应采样策略，实现了人形机器人的运动跟踪与跌倒恢复，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将运动跟踪和跌倒恢复视为不同任务，需要多阶段训练和专门的恢复奖励/策略，且训练时会在严重跟踪失败后立即终止，限制了在失稳或跌倒状态下的恢复探索。

Method: 1) 偏航对齐的跟踪表示（减少全局漂移和航向干扰的敏感性，保留重力相关平衡信息）；2) 基于伯努利分布的概率终止机制（鼓励探索不同故障模式下的跌倒恢复行为）；3) 概率终止与跟踪误差驱动的自适应采样策略（根据跟踪性能动态重塑采样分布，提高困难动作段和不稳定状态的训练效率）。

Result: 与SOTA方法的广泛对比和消融实验表明，Stubborn取得了具有竞争力的性能，概率终止机制和自适应采样策略对性能和鲁棒性提升有贡献。

Conclusion: Stubborn通过统一的框架有效解决了人形机器人运动跟踪与跌倒恢复问题，验证了概率终止机制和自适应采样策略的有效性。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have shown great promise in improving humanoid motion tracking performance and achieving fall recovery under disturbances. However, most existing works treat motion tracking and fall recovery as different tasks and require multi-stage training with specialized recovery rewards and/or separate recovery policies. Moreover, existing reinforcement learning-based methods often terminate training episodes immediately after severe tracking failures, limiting recovery-oriented exploration in unstable or fallen states. To address the above issues, we propose Stubborn, a streamlined and unified reinforcement learning framework to achieve robust humanoid motion tracking and fall recovery. Specifically, Stubborn uses an asymmetric Actor-Critic architecture and consists of three major components. First, a yaw-aligned tracking representation is adopted to reduce sensitivity to global drift and heading disturbances while preserving gravity-related balance information. Second, we introduce a Bernoulli-based probabilistic termination mechanism that enables the policy to encourage exploration of fall-recovery behaviors under varying failure modes. Third, we propose a probabilistic termination and tracking-error-driven strategy that dynamically reshapes the sampling distribution based on tracking performance, increasing the training efficiency for difficult motion segments and unstable states. Extensive comparisons with SOTA methods and ablation studies show that Stubborn achieved competitive performance, and the proposed probabilistic termination mechanism and adaptive sampling strategy contributed to the performance and robustness gains. For real-world demonstrations, please refer to https://aislab-sustech.github.io/Stubborn/.

</details>


### [174] [AIR-VLA+: Decoupling Movement and Manipulation via Cascaded Dual-Action Decoders with Asymmetric MoE for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12859)
*Jianli Sun,Bin Tian,Qiyao Zhang,Zijian Liu,Yutong Wang,Zhiyong Cui,Bai Li,Yisheng Lv,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AIR-VLA+提出了一种针对空中操作的流匹配动作生成架构，通过级联双动作解码器和非对称特征级MoE，解耦无人机移动与机械臂操作的控制，在标准化基准上以48.0总分超越所有基线，任务完成分数相比单头策略提升80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作系统中端到端控制存在严重的表征耦合问题：无人机平台级移动与末端执行器级臂操作在动作尺度、动力学和控制目标上差异巨大，导致异构协调控制冲突。

Method: 1) 级联双动作解码器：操纵解码器与移动解码器级联，使无人机单向观察机械臂意图实现协调，同时隔离移动信息反向传播对臂稳定性的影响；2) 输入特征增强模块：隐式视觉抓取投影仪感知夹爪与物体交互状态，注入压缩全局语义特征；3) 隐式MoE架构：在移动解码器中部署，使不同移动专家在训练中自发表现出对不同任务阶段的能力倾向，通过特征流形的密集软融合计算增强任务阶段适应性。

Result: 在AIR-VLA标准化基准上，方法以48.0平均分全面超越所有基线，任务完成分数比单头π0.5策略提升80.2%，有效缓解了复合机器人的异构协调控制冲突。

Conclusion: 级联解耦与非对称MoE设计有效解决了空中操作中移动与操作的动作表征耦合问题，为复合机器人异构协调控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation systems have long suffered from representation coupling in end-to-end control, as platform-level Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) movement and end-effector-level arm manipulation differ substantially in action scale, dynamics, and control objectives. In this paper, we propose AIR-VLA+, a flow matching action generation architecture specifically designed for aerial manipulation, featuring cascaded dual-action decoders and an asymmetric feature-level Mixture of Experts (MoE). We construct cascaded manipulation and movement decoders, allowing the UAV to unidirectionally observe the manipulator's intent during movement to achieve workflow coordination, while isolating the impact of UAV movement information backpropagation on arm manipulation stability. Addressing the characteristic that UAV movement is highly dependent on high-level semantics and responsible for task state transitions in aerial manipulation, we design an input feature enhancement module for the UAV movement decoder. This module introduces an implicit visual grasp projector to perceive the interaction state between the gripper and the object, and injects compressed global semantic features. Within the UAV movement decoder, we deploy an implicit MoE architecture, enabling different movement experts to spontaneously exhibit capacity inclinations for various task stages during training. Through dense soft blending computation on the feature manifold, the UAV movement is endowed with stronger task-stage adaptability. Experiments on the standardized AIR-VLA benchmark demonstrate that our method comprehensively surpasses all baselines with an overall average score of 48.0. The overall task completion score improves by 80.2\% compared to the single-head $π_{0.5}$ policy, effectively mitigating the heterogeneous coordinated control conflicts of composite robots.

</details>


### [175] [Learning to Adapt: Representation-Based Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Task Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12890)
*Aryan Naveen,Haitong Ma,Haldun Balim,Na Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RepMT-SAC多任务强化学习框架，通过谱MDP分解实现跨任务知识共享，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习存在样本效率低和跨任务泛化能力差的局限性，亟需一种能高效共享知识并迁移到新任务的框架。

Method: 采用谱MDP分解捕捉可迁移的动力学特征，将价值函数结构化为任务无关核心+最小任务特定调整项。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，分布内任务零样本表现优异，分布外任务可快速少样本适应，比基线提升高达30%。

Conclusion: RepMT-SAC框架通过结构化知识共享有效解决了多任务RL中的泛化与迁移问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in learning complex control policies, yet its applicability remains limited due to sample inefficiency and poor generalization across tasks. In this work, we propose RepMT-SAC, a framework for multi-task RL that enables efficient knowledge sharing and robust transfer to new tasks. RepMT-SAC uses spectral MDP decomposition to capture transferable dynamics, structuring the value function into a task-agnostic core with a minimal task-specific adjustment. This design allows for strong zero-shot performance on in-distribution tasks and rapid few-shot adaptation to out-of-distribution tasks. We evaluate RepMT-SAC on quadcopter trajectory-following tasks across in-distribution and out-of-distribution contexts, demonstrating that it outperforms baselines by up to 30%.

</details>


### [176] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个轻量级框架，利用预训练VLM将自然语言指令转化为神经符号目标状态，实现开词汇表桌面操作，无需微调即可理解抽象空间概念，在90次真实机器人试验中达到73.3%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的机器人任务规划方法要么计算量大（heavyweight），要么需要大量演示数据进行训练，缺乏高效、零样本的开放词汇操作方案。

Method: 利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询翻译为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管道将符号状态 grounding 到物理世界，无需固定颜色列表或硬编码坐标，支持"top shelf"等抽象空间概念。

Result: 在90次真实机器人试试验中，三个难度级别总体成功率达73.3%，且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP证明了利用预训练VLM实现高效、零样本的开放词汇桌面操作是可行的，为机器人理解抽象空间概念并执行任务提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline.
